Implement top-level globals for log-LSE L-BFGS:
```
_LOG_LSE_BETA=100
_LOG_LSE_MASK=None
_LOG_LSE_ALPHA=1.0
_LOG_LSE_N=0
_LOG_LSE_MASK_ALL=True

def softmax_vector(y):
    m = np.max(y)
    e = np.exp(y-m)
    e[e<1e-300]=0? np.exp underflows to 0. e += tiny? ensure sum.
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(len(y), 1/len(y))
    return e/s

def log_lse_obj_grad(y):
    n = _LOG_LSE_N
    y = np.asarray(y)
    a = softmax_vector(y)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if mask is None: vals = c
    else: vals = np.where(mask, c, -np.inf)
    cmax = np.max(vals[np.isfinite(vals)])
    scaled = _LOG_LSE_BETA * (vals - cmax)
    scaled_f = scaled - np.max(scaled) ? Need for -inf.
    finite_mask = np.isfinite(scaled)
    if not any: return large, zeros
    scaled_f = scaled[finite]
    scaled_f -= scaled_f.max()
    ex = np.exp(scaled_f)
    sum_ex = ex.sum()
    L = cmax + np.log(sum_ex)/beta
    p = np.zeros(len(vals)); p[finite] = ex/sum_ex
    r = crosscorr_rev(a,p)
    grad_a = 2.0 * _LOG_LSE_ALPHA * r  # alpha
    g = a * (grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
    # If nonfinite, fallback
    return _LOG_LSE_ALPHA * L, g
```

In `_log_lse_obj`:
```
def _log_lse_objective(y):
    n = _LOG_LSE_N
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    # prevent huge magnitude
    y = np.clip(y, -100.0, 100.0)  # gradient not exact at clip but bounded; okay? Could cause flat.
    a = softmax(y)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if _LOG_LSE_MASK is not None:
       vals = c.copy(); vals[~_LOG_LSE_MASK] = -np.inf
    else: vals = c
    finite = np.isfinite(vals)
    if not finite.any(): return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
    vmax = np.max(vals[finite])
    scaled = np.full_like(c, -1e300)
    scaled[finite] = _LOG_LSE_BETA * (vals[finite] - vmax)
    scaled_f = scaled - np.max(scaled) # -inf issues; better subset
    sf = scaled[finite]
    sf -= sf.max()
    ex = np.exp(sf)
    # if ex all zero? sf max 0 so at least one 1.
    p = np.zeros(len(c))
    p[finite] = ex / ex.sum()
    L = vmax / beta? Wait L = vmax + log(sum exp(beta(vals-vmax)))/beta. Compute `lse = vmax + np.log(ex.sum())/beta`.
    # r
    r = crosscorr_a_p(a,p)
    grad_a = 2.0 * r  # times alpha? alpha=1
    # objective f = 2*n? We can use lse (scale independent)
    obj = lse
    g = a * (grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
    # Clean
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(obj): return 1e12, zeros
    return obj, g
```
Check softmax gradient formula with f(a)=L(a). We computed grad_y = a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)). Correct.
- If we clip y to [-100,100], gradient at boundary not exact but objective finite; L-BFGS may get stuck at boundary. Instead of clipping in objective, we can avoid clipping and handle overflow: `m=np.max(y); z=y-m; e=np.exp(z); e[z<-745]=0` (numpy does). Sum could underflow if all z very negative? max z=0 so at least one exp(0)=1. Good. y can be large but max subtraction stable. If y has inf? L-BFGS may produce inf; handle: if not all finite, replace with 0. Use `np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)`. If y range > 2000, some zero, fine. No need clip. Gradient for underflow zeros may be zero due a=0; but if y large negative for many, a=0, gradient zero; they might never increase if directional? L-BFGS may not recover. Initial y from parent with small eps not too extreme. Good.

Crosscorr A,P:
```
def crosscorr_a_p(a,p):
    n=len(a); lenp=len(p); M=n+lenp-1; Mf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=Mf); P=np.fft.rfft(p,n=Mf)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*P,M=Mf)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return r
```
For n=1, M=1? If n=1, p len1, M=1, Mf=1; slice [0:1] ok. But fast_conv length1.

`fast_conv(a)` for n=1: M=1, Mf=1, rfft length1, irfft length1, ok.

def make_mask(n, type):
    L=2*n-1
    mask = np.ones(L, bool)
    center=n-1
    if type == 'no_center': mask[center]=False
    elif type == 'no_center_w3': start=max(0,center-1); end=min(L,center+2); mask[start:end]=False
    elif type == 'no_center_w16': start=max(0,center-8); end=min(L,center+9); mask[start:end]=False
    elif type == 'no_edges': mask[0]=False; mask[-1]=False
    elif type == 'no_edges_center': ...
    return mask
```

Now, peak shaving top-level:
```
def shave_peak_candidate(a, mask, rng, alpha=0.05, top_lags=64):
    an = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    n=len(an)
    c=fast_conv(an)
    if mask is not None: vals=np.where(mask,c,-np.inf)
    else: vals=c
    finite=np.isfinite(vals)
    if not finite.any(): return an
    thresh=np.quantile(vals[finite], max(0.9, 1 - 1.0/max(1,top_lags)/max(1,finite.sum()))) maybe.
    w=np.zeros_like(c)
    w[finite] = np.maximum(vals[finite]-thresh,0)
    if w.sum()<=0: return an
    # normalize weights to mean maybe
    w = w / w.sum()
    # importance r_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}
    r = crosscorr_a_p(an, w)
    # We want reduce high r, add to low r.
    delta = alpha * np.maximum(r - np.mean(r), 0.0)
    total = delta.sum()
    if total <=1e-15: return an
    new=an - delta
    new=np.maximum(new,0)
    # low mask where new small? or r below mean
    low = r < np.quantile(r,0.3)
    if not low.any(): low = np.tile([True,False],n//2+1)*? 
    total_new = new.sum()
    if total_new < 1e-12: return an
    # add to low proportional to (low_weight)
    add_w = an[low] + 1e-12
    new[low] += total * add_w / add_w.sum()
    return normalize_sum(new)
```
This reduces weighted peaks.

def choose_mask(evaluated, parent_len):
    # evaluated list of tuples (a, score, n,S)
    mask_defs = [('full', None), ('no_center', 'no_center'), ...]
    best_name='full'; best_cv=None
    for name in names:
        ratios=[]
        for a,score,n,S in evaluated:
            c=fast_conv(a)
            mask = make_mask(n,name)
            mval = metric_max(c, mask)
            if mval > 1e-12 and score > 1e-12:
                ratios.append(score*S*S/mval)
        if len(ratios)>=3:
            r=np.array(ratios); med=np.median(r); cv=np.std(r)/ (med+1e-12)
            if cv < best_cv: ...
    # special: if uniform full score near 2 and best_score >=1.9: return 'full'
    # if spike with no_center exact low exists, return 'no_center'
    return best_name

CG top-level details:
```
def cg_solve_conv_derivative(a, residual, weights=None, iters=30, reg=1e-10):
    n=len(a)
    x=np.zeros(n)

Let's work out CG step in detail with functions:
```
def apply_M_conv(a, d): return 2.0 * fast_conv2(a, d)
def apply_Mt(a, y): return 2.0 * crosscorr_a_p(a, y)

def cg_flattening_step(a, T, weights=None, iters=20, reg=1e-12):
    n=len(a)
    x=np.zeros(n)
    b=apply_Mt(a, (weights* (T - fast_conv(a))) if weights is not None else (T - fast_conv(a)))
    b -= b.mean()
    if np.dot(b,b) < 1e-30: return np.zeros(n)
    r=b.copy()
    p=r.copy()
    rn1=np.dot(r,r)
    for it in range(iters):
        y=apply_M_conv(a,p)
        zy = weights*y if weights is not None else y
        v=apply_Mt(a, zy)
        v -= v.mean()  # project
        num=np.dot(r,p)
        den=np.dot(p,v) + reg*np.dot(p,p)
        if den <= 1e-30: break
        alpha=num/den
        x = x + alpha*p
        x -= x.mean()
        r = r - alpha*v
        rn2=np.dot(r,r)
        if rn2 < 1e-20: break
        r -= r.mean()
        p = r + (rn2/rn1)*p
        p -= p.mean()
        rn1=rn2
    return x
```
But standard CG: `r_new = r - alpha v`; `beta = rn2/rn1`; `p_new = r_new + beta p`. Here rn1 old `r·r`. We update rn1=rn2. Good.
- Need weights nonnegative length 2n-1. Uniform None. If using LSE weights, normalized sum1; scaling doesn't matter for V but b scaled; alpha? Scaling weights scales both b and v, output same? b scales w, V scales w, r scales w; denom scales w²? num scales w? Actually r ~ w, v ~ w, den ~w², num ~w? alpha inverse w; x same because r and V scaled. Good.
- For T: We want target less than current max. Use `T = maxc * t_factor`. But if t_factor close 1, residual small. We can set `T = (maxc*0.5 + mean? )`? Let's use iterative t_factor decreasing: [0.995,0.99,0.98,0.96,0.92] maybe. But if T too low, CG may change a too much. Line search protects.
- We can choose weights emphasizing peaks: `w = np.exp((c - maxc) / (maxc - meanc + 1e-18))`? This downweights low lags, focuses on reducing peaks; equalization maybe uniform better. Could use `w = np.ones` for first step, then `w = np.maximum(1 - c/maxc, 0.05)`? If peaks high, weights low; no. Use uniform.

Line search after δ:
```
def line_search_delta(a, delta, candidates_tau=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15,0.1)):
    best_a=a; best_sc=internal_score(a)
    # maybe cap delta relative
    for tau in candidates_tau:
       at = a + tau*delta
       at = np.clip(at,0,None)
       if at.sum()<1e-15: continue
       at = at/at.sum()
       sc = internal_score(at)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=at
    return best_a, best_sc
```
This uses internal score not exact. For final, exact check.
- To avoid destroying positivity, before line search clip delta: `max_inc = 0.5*a + 1e-6; min_dec = -0.5*a - 1e-6; delta = np.clip(delta, min_dec, max_inc)`.Tau up to 1 then change ≤50% coordinates.

Run CG sequence:
```
def cg_polish(a, t_factors, cg_iters, deadline):
   best=a; best_sc=internal_score(a)
   for tf in t_factors:
      if time > deadline - 30: break
      c=fast_conv(best); T=c.max()*tf
      delta=cg_step(best, T, iters)
      # maybe multiple line steps: accept if internal improves, repeat for same tf up to 2 times
      for _ in range(2):
         cand, sc = line_search_delta(best, delta)
         if not improved: break
         best=cand; best_sc=sc
      # optionally recompute delta if improved?
   return best,best_sc
```

def block_descent_full(a, block_size=256, scales=(0.2,0.5,0.75,0.9,1.1,1.3,1.7,2.2), sweeps=4, deadline=None):
    a = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a; best_sc=internal_score_full(a)
    for sw in range(sweeps):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline-60: break
       for s in range(0,n,block_size):
          e=min(s+block_size,n)
          # compute candidate base by scaling current a? Use current a or best_a? Use current a for coordinate.
          base_a=a[:].copy()
          cur_sc=best_e? We need internal score of current a.
          local_best = a; local_sc = current_sc
          for f in scales:
             cand = base_a.copy()
             cand[s:e] *= f
             cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
             ss=cand.sum(); if ss<1e-12: continue
             cand/=ss
             sc=internal_score_full(cand)
             if sc<local_sc:
                local_sc=sc; local_best=cand
          if local_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=local_sc; best_a=local_best
          a = local_best
       # optional shuffle starts if deadline? no
    return best_a,best_sc
```
This copies many arrays; OK for 7000*1000 ~70M memory operations. Could optimize by prealloc but fine.
- Use current `a` as base for each block; if no change factor, local_best=a. The current_sc should be internal_score of current a; update after block. Cache.

Forward LSE:
```
def lse_forward(a, beta):
    n=len(a)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # stable
    scaled=scaled - np.max(scaled) # all finite because c finite
    ex=np.exp(scaled)
    s=ex.sum()
    if s<1e-300: p=np.full(len(c),1.0/len(c))
    else: p=ex/s
    L=cmax + np.log(s)/beta if s>0 else cmax
    return L,p
```
Since c finite, no -inf. For full all lags. No mask needed now (full objective). Great; we can remove mask complexity. The prompt objective full, no need masks. Our earlier spike probes irrelevant. But keep uniform maybe.
- If we want side? no.

Symmetric expansion:
```
def expand_sym_y(y):
    n = _LOG_LSE_NFULL
    m = _LOG_LSE_NVAR
    full = np.empty(n)
    full[:m]=y
    full[m:] = y[:n-m][::-1]
    return full

def contract_grad_sym(gfull):
    n=_LOG_LSE_NFULL; m=_LOG_LSE_NVAR
    g = gfull[:m].copy()
    g[:n-m] += gfull[m:][::-1]
    return g
```
Check for even n=4,m=2,n-m=2: g0+=gfull0, +gfull3? gfull[m:]=g2,g3 reversed [g3,g2]; g[:2]+= [g3,g2]. So g0=g0+g3, g1=g1+g2 correct. Odd n=5,m=3,n-m=2: gfull[3:]=g3,g4 reversed [g4,g3]; g2=middle not added -> g2=g2 correct. Good.

In objective sym, `full_y=expand_sym_y(y); a=softmax_vector(full_y);` etc.

`run_log_lbfgs`:
```
def run_log_lbfgs(a0, beta, symmetric=False, maxiter=None, maxfun=None):
    a0=np.clip(a0,0,None); a0=a0/ a0.sum()
    n=len(a0)
    m = n//2 if n%2==0 else n//2+1
    if symmetric:
       a0 = symmetrize(a0)
    global _...
    y0=np.log(a0+1e-300)
    if symmetric:
       # use representative m: a0[:m]; but if a0 all equal? okay.
       y0 = y0[:m]
    _LOG_LSE_BETA=float(beta); _LOG_LSE_SYMM=symmetric; _LOG_LSE_NFULL=n; _LOG_LSE_NVAR=m; _LOG_LSE_YMAX=30.0
    bounds=[(-_LOG_LSE_YMAX,_LOG_LSE_YMAX)]*m
    if maxiter is None: maxiter=800 if n>4096 else 2000
    if maxfun is None: maxfun=3000 if n>4096 else 10000
    res=minimize(log_lse_objective, y0, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-14,'maxls':50})
    y=np.clip(res.x, -_LOG_LSE_YMAX, _LOG_LSE_YMAX)
    full_y = expand_sym_y(y) if symmetric else y
    a=softmax_vector(full_y)
    return normalize_sum(a)
```
Need ensure `expand_sym_y` uses globals set. Good.
- If n large, `bounds` list of tuples length 3500/7000 memory okay.
- If scipy not available? allowed.

`softmax_vector(y)`:
```
def softmax_vector(y):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
       y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    m=np.max(y)
    e=np.exp(y-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(len(y), 1.0/len(y))
    return e/s
```
Good.

`fast_conv2(x,y)`:
- lengths nx,ny. total nx+ny-1. M=1 << (total-1).bit_length() but if total=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. Use `max(1, 1 << (total-1).bit_length())`.
- `rx=np.fft.rfft(x, M); ry=np.fft.rfft(y, M); out=np.fft.irfft(rx*ry, M)[:total]`. Ensure real.

`fast_conv(a)`: call fast_conv2(a,a) maybe compute twice? We can optimize square: `A=np.fft.rfft(a,M); irfft(A*A)`. Keep separate. Implementation:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a); total=2*n-1
    M=1 << (total-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:total]
```
For n=0? not.

def two_block_search(base, splits_fractions=(0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75), factors=(0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2,1.7,2.5,4.0)):
   n=len(base); best_a=base; best_sc=internal_score_1(base)
   for sf in splits:
      m=int(sf*n)
      for fL in factors:
        for fR in factors:
           cand=base.copy(); cand[:m]*=fL; cand[m:]*=fR; normalize; sc=internal_score_1(cand); update
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Now, exact safe evaluation:
```
def safe_eval(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: a = a * (0.01/s) if s>0 else np.ones(a.size)*0.01/a.size
    # maybe if length 0 return inf
    seq=[float(x) for x in a]
    try: val=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception: val=np.inf
    return val
```

def block_descent_multi(a, sizes=(64,256,1024), sweeps=2, deadline):

Top-level `random_shape_search(base, rng, n_trials=120, keep=8)`:
```
n=len(base); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
top_scores=[]; top_seqs=[]
for t in range(n_trials):
   kind = int(rng.integers(0,6))
   cand=base.copy()
   if kind==0: exponent=rng.uniform(0.3,2.0); cand=np.power(np.maximum(base,1e-12), exponent)
   elif kind==1: slope=rng.uniform(-2.0,3.0); cand*=np.exp(slope*(x-0.5))
   elif kind==2: gamma=rng.uniform(-3.0,3.0); cand*=np.exp(gamma*(x-0.5)**2)
   elif kind==3: split=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); fL=rng.uniform(0.3,2.5); fR=rng.uniform(0.3,2.5); multiply blocks
   elif kind==4: amt=rng.uniform(0.05,2.0); cand[0]+=amt; cand[-1]+=amt
   else: # sinusoidal
      amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.6); freq=rng.uniform(1,5); ph=...; cand*= (1+amp*np.cos(2πfreqx+ph))
   cand=normalize_sum(np.maximum(cand,0)); sc=internal_score_1(cand); update top.
return list of (score,seq)
```
No lambda. This may find good shapes quickly. Exact top few.

`log_lse_objective`: Need ensure y length m. Use `_LOG_LSE_NVAR` maybe. If not sym, m=nfull.
```
def log_lse_objective(y):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
    if not all finite: y=np.where(isfinite,y,0)
    y=np.clip(y, -_LOG_LSE_YMAX, _LOG_LSE_YMAX)
    if _LOG_LSE_SYMM:
       full_y=expand_sym_y(y)
    else:
       full_y=y
    a=softmax_vector(full_y)
    L,p=lse_forward(a,_LOG_LSE_BETA)
    r=crosscorr_a_p(a,p)
    dL=2.0*r
    gfull=a*(dL - float(np.dot(a,dL)))
    if _LOG_LSE_SYMM: g=contract_grad_sym(gfull)
    else: g=gfull
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(L): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(g)
    return L,g
```

r=b.copy(); p=r.copy(); rn1=r.r
for it:
  v=V(p)
  num=r.p; den=p.v + reg*p.p
  if den<=0: break
  alpha=num/den
  x += alpha p
  r -= alpha v
  rn2=r.r
  if rn2<1e-16: break
  beta=rn2/rn1
  p = r + beta p
  rn1=rn2
```

`full_pipeline` details:
```
def full_pipeline(a0, rng, deadline):
    best=normalize_sum(a0.copy())
    best_sc=internal_score_1(best)
    # deterministic two-block/taper early
    cand, sc = two_block_search(best, deadline)
    update
    # random shape search
    for sc,cand in random_shape_search(best, rng, 120, 10):
        if sc<best_sc: update
    # block descent multi
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 240:
        cand,sc=block_descent_multi(best, deadline)
        update
    # cg
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        cand,sc=cg_flatten_sequence(best, deadline)
        update
    # log lbfgs
    betas=[1000,5000,20000]
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180:
            cand=run_log_lbfgs(best,beta)
            sc=internal_score_1(cand); update
    # symmetric LBFGS
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        cand=run_log_lbfgs(best, 20000, symmetric=True)
        sc=internal_score_1(cand); update
    # second block + cg
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        cand,sc=block_descent_multi(best, deadline)
        update
        if time < deadline - 30:
            cand,sc=cg_flatten_sequence(best, deadline)
    return best,best_sc
```
Time thresholds may be too optimistic. `run_log_lbfgs` for n=7000 maxfun 2000 ~20s; thresholds 180 enough. two_block 1s. block multi 10s. cg 1s. Should be fine. If n large 16k, times larger; but main n ≤12288. We can set conservative.

def two_block_search(base, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_sum(base.copy())
    best=a; best_sc=internal_score_1(a)
    n=len(a)
    splits=(0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7)
    factors=(0.3,0.6,0.9,1.25,1.7,2.5)
    for sf in splits:
       m=int(sf*n); if m<=0 or m>=n: continue
       for fL in factors:
          for fR in factors:
             if fL==1.0 and fR==1.0: continue
             cand=a.copy(); cand[:m]*=fL; cand[m:]*=fR
             cand=normalize_sum(cand)
             sc=internal_score_1(cand)
             if sc < best_sc - 1e-15: best_sc=sc; best=cand
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline-120: break
    return best,best_sc
```

def cg_flattening_step(a,T,iters=8,reg=1e-12):
   c=fast_conv(a)
   r=T - c
   b=apply_Mt(a,r)
   if np.dot(b,b) < 1e-30: return np.zeros_like(a)
   x=np.zeros_like(a); p=b.copy(); r_cg=b.copy(); rn1=np.dot(r_cg,r_cg)
   for it in range(iters):
      y=apply_M_conv(a,p)
      v=apply_Mt(a,y)
      num=np.dot(r_cg,p)
      den=np.dot(p,v) + reg*np.dot(p,p)
      if not np.isfinite(den) or den <= 1e-30: break
      alpha=num/den
      if not np.isfinite(alpha): break
      x = x + alpha*p
      r_cg = r_cg - alpha*v
      rn2=np.dot(r_cg,r_cg)
      if rn2 < 1e-24: break
      beta=rn2/rn1
      p = r_cg + beta*p
      rn1=rn2
   return x
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences a.

Strategy:
- Use a strong analytic seed: the positive coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2),
  whose infinite self-convolution is flat.
- Improve it with deterministic block scaling, random low-dimensional shape
  search, a Gauss-Newton-style flattening step, and L-BFGS in log-softmax
  space with a log-sum-exp smoothed maximum.
- If a previous sequence is available, use it as another seed and choose the
  best verified sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

LOG_LSE_BETA = 100.0
LOG_LSE_YMAX = 30.0
LOG_LSE_SYMMETRIC = False
LOG_LSE_NFULL = 0
LOG_LSE_NVAR = 0


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-18:
        n = a.size if a.size > 0 else 1
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)


def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    total = 2 * n - 1
    m = next_pow2(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:total]


def fast_conv2(x, y):
    total = len(x) + len(y) - 1
    m = next_pow2(total)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, m)
    Y = np.fft.rfft(y, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(X * Y, m)[:total]


def crosscorr_a_p(a, p):
    """
    Return r[i] = sum_k p[k] a[k-i] for valid k, i.
    Used for gradients of convolution coefficients.
    """
    n = len(a)
    total = n + len(p) - 1
    m = next_pow2(total)
    rev_a = a[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rev_a, m)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    out = np.fft.irfft(R * P, m)[:total]
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def internal_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-18:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def internal_score_1(a):
    """Score for a normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-18:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * float(np.max(fast_conv(a))))


def build_flat_prefix(n):
    """
    Positive coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n.
    Its *infinite* self-convolution is flat, so its finite prefix is a
    natural non-uniform seed.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    return normalize_sum(a)


def symmetrize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    return normalize_sum(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))


def safe_eval(a):
    """Call the external evaluate_sequence with a plain Python list."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        if a.size == 0:
            a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a * (0.02 / max(1e-300, s))
    seq = [float(x) for x in a]
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def softmax_vector(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    m = float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(y.shape, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def lse_forward(a, beta):
    c = fast_conv(a)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = beta * (c - cm)
    scaled -= float(np.max(scaled))
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        L = cm
    else:
        p = e / s
        L = cm + float(np.log(s)) / float(beta)
    return L, p


def expand_sym_y(y):
    """Expand a symmetric log-variable vector to the full sequence length."""
    n = int(LOG_LSE_NFULL)
    m = int(LOG_LSE_NVAR)
    full = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    full[:m] = y
    full[m:] = y[:n - m][::-1]
    return full


def contract_grad_sym(gfull):
    n = int(LOG_LSE_NFULL)
    m = int(LOG_LSE_NVAR)
    g = gfull[:m].copy()
    g[:n - m] += gfull[m:][::-1]
    return g


def log_lse_objective(y):
    """
    Log-sum-exp smoothed objective and gradient with respect to log-softmax
    parameters. Global state is set by run_log_lbfgs().
    """
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -LOG_LSE_YMAX, LOG_LSE_YMAX)

    if LOG_LSE_SYMMETRIC:
        full_y = expand_sym_y(y)
    else:
        full_y = y

    a = softmax_vector(full_y)
    L, p = lse_forward(a, LOG_LSE_BETA)
    r = crosscorr_a_p(a, p)
    dL_da = 2.0 * r
    gfull = a * (dL_da - float(np.dot(a, dL_da)))

    if LOG_LSE_SYMMETRIC:
        g = contract_grad_sym(gfull)
    else:
        g = gfull

    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(L):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(g)
    return L, g


def run_log_lbfgs(a0, beta, symmetric=False, maxiter=None, maxfun=None):
    """L-BFGS in log-softmax space."""
    a0 = normalize_sum(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    if symmetric:
        a0 = symmetrize(a0)

    m = (n // 2) if (n % 2 == 0) else (n // 2 + 1)

    global LOG_LSE_BETA, LOG_LSE_YMAX, LOG_LSE_SYMMETRIC, LOG_LSE_NFULL, LOG_LSE_NVAR
    LOG_LSE_BETA = float(beta)
    LOG_LSE_YMAX = 30.0
    LOG_LSE_SYMMETRIC = bool(symmetric)
    LOG_LSE_NFULL = int(n)
    LOG_LSE_NVAR = int(m)

    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    if symmetric:
        y0 = y0[:m]
    else:
        y0 = y0.copy()

    bounds = [(-LOG_LSE_YMAX, LOG_LSE_YMAX)] * m

    if maxiter is None:
        if n > 10000:
            maxiter = 400
        elif n > 6000:
            maxiter = 600
        elif n > 2000:
            maxiter = 1200
        else:
            maxiter = 3000

    if maxfun is None:
        if n > 10000:
            maxfun = 600
        elif n > 6000:
            maxfun = 1200
        elif n > 2000:
            maxfun = 4000
        else:
            maxfun = 15000

    try:
        res = minimize(
            log_lse_objective,
            y0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            jac=True,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-18,
                "gtol": 1e-14,
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        y = y0

    y = np.clip(y, -LOG_LSE_YMAX, LOG_LSE_YMAX)
    if symmetric:
        full_y = expand_sym_y(y)
    else:
        full_y = y
    a = softmax_vector(full_y)
    return normalize_sum(a)


def two_block_search(base, deadline=None):
    """Searchinhomogeneous left/right scaling."""
    a = normalize_sum(base)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)
    n = len(a)

    splits = (0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70)
    factors = (0.35, 0.60, 0.85, 1.25, 1.75, 2.50)

    for sf in splits:
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 120.0):
            break
        m = int(round(sf * n))
        if m <= 0 or m >= n:
            continue
        for fl in factors:
            for fr in factors:
                if fl == 1.0 and fr == 1.0:
                    continue
                cand = a.copy()
                cand[:m] *= fl
                cand[m:] *= fr
                cand = normalize_sum(cand)
                sc = internal_score_1(cand)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def random_shape_search(base, rng, n_trials=120, keep=8):
    """Random smooth low-dimensional transformations of a base sequence."""
    a0 = normalize_sum(base)
    n = len(a0)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    top_scores = []
    top_seqs = []

    for _ in range(int(n_trials)):
        a = a0.copy()
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

        if kind == 0:
            p = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 2.20))
            a = np.power(np.maximum(a0, 1e-12), p)
        elif kind == 1:
            slope = float(rng.uniform(-2.2, 3.2))
            a *= np.exp(slope * (x - 0.5))
        elif kind == 2:
            gamma = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
            a *= np.exp(gamma * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
        elif kind == 3:
            split = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
            m = int(round(split * n))
            fl = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5))
            fr = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5))
            if 0 < m < n:
                a[:m] *= fl
                a[m:] *= fr
        elif kind == 4:
            amt = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 2.0))
            a[0] += amt
            if n > 1:
                a[-1] += amt
        else:
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.65))
            freq = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a *= (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))

        a = normalize_sum(np.maximum(a, 0.0))
        sc = internal_score_1(a)

        if len(top_scores) < keep:
            top_scores.append(sc)
            top_seqs.append(a.copy())
        else:
            mi = 0
            mv = top_scores[0]
            for i in range(1, len(top_scores)):
                if top_scores[i] < mv:
                    mv = top_scores[i]
                    mi = i
            if sc < mv:
                top_scores[mi] = sc
                top_seqs[mi] = a.copy()

    out = []
    for s, a in zip(top_scores, top_seqs):
        out.append((float(s), a))
    return out


def block_descent_full(a, block_size=128, scales=(0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0),
                       sweeps=2, deadline=None):
    """Coordinate descent over multiplicatively scaled contiguous blocks."""
    a = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)
    current = a.copy()
    current_sc = best_sc

    for _ in range(int(sweeps)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 120.0):
            break
        for s in range(0, n, int(block_size)):
            e = min(n, s + int(block_size))
            local_best = current
            local_sc = current_sc
            base = current.copy()

            for f in scales:
                if f == 1.0:
                    continue
                cand = base.copy()
                cand[s:e] *= float(f)
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                cand = normalize_sum(cand)
                sc = internal_score_1(cand)
                if sc < local_sc - 1e-15:
                    local_sc = sc
                    local_best = cand

            current = local_best
            current_sc = local_sc
            if current_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = current_sc
                best = current.copy()

            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 60.0):
                break
    return best, best_sc


def block_descent_multi(a, deadline):
    best = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)
    n = len(best)

    sizes = []
    for s in (64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
        if 4 <= s < n and s not in sizes:
            sizes.append(s)

    for i, bs in enumerate(sizes[:3]):
        if time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 180.0):
            break
        if i == 0:
            sweeps = 2
        elif i == 1:
            sweeps = 2
        else:
            sweeps = 1
        cand, sc = block_descent_full(best, block_size=bs, sweeps=sweeps, deadline=deadline)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def apply_M_conv(a, d):
    return 2.0 * fast_conv2(a, d)


def apply_Mt(a, y):
    return 2.0 * crosscorr_a_p(a, y)


def cg_flattening_step(a, T, iters=8, reg=1e-12):
    """
    One Gauss-Newton-style step for reducing max convolution:
    solve min || M delta - (T - c) || with M delta = 2(a*delta).
    """
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    r = T - c
    b = apply_Mt(a, r)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    bnn = float(np.dot(b, b))
    if bnn < 1e-30:
        return np.zeros_like(a)

    x = np.zeros_like(a)
    r_cg = b.copy()
    p = r_cg.copy()
    rn1 = float(np.dot(r_cg, r_cg))

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        y = apply_M_conv(a, p)
        v = apply_Mt(a, y)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            break
        num = float(np.dot(r_cg, p))
        den = float(np.dot(p, v)) + float(reg) * float(np.dot(p, p))
        if not np.isfinite(den) or den <= 1e-30:
            break
        alpha = num / den
        if not np.isfinite(alpha) or alpha == 0.0:
            break
        x += alpha * p
        r_cg -= alpha * v
        rn2 = float(np.dot(r_cg, r_cg))
        if not np.isfinite(rn2) or rn2 < 1e-24:
            break
        beta = rn2 / rn1
        p = r_cg + beta * p
        rn1 = rn2

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    return x


def line_search_delta(a, delta, taus=(1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1)):
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)

    for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
        d = sign * delta
        for t in taus:
            cand = a + float(t) * d
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            ss = float(cand.sum())
            if ss < 1e-18:
                continue
            cand = cand / ss
            sc = internal_score_1(cand)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = sc
                best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def cg_flatten_sequence(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)

    fracs = (0.50, 0.30, 0.75, 0.90)
    for frac in fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 120.0):
            break
        c = fast_conv(best)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
        mn = float(np.mean(c))
        T = mn + float(frac) * (mx - mn)
        for _ in range(2):
            delta = cg_flattening_step(best, T, iters=8)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
                break
            delta = np.clip(delta, -0.5 * best - 1e-10, 1.0 * best + 1e-10)
            cand, sc = line_search_delta(best, delta)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = cand
                best_sc = sc
            else:
                break
    return best, best_sc


def micro_peak_sweep(a, top_lags=12):
    """Final local coordinate polishing around worst lags."""
    a = normalize_sum(a.copy())
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    peaks = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:int(top_lags)]
    factors = (0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.33)

    for k in peaks:
        k = int(k)
        l = max(0, k - n + 1)
        r = min(k, n - 1)
        if r < l:
            continue
        idxs = np.arange(l, r + 1)
        jdxs = k - idxs
        contrib = a[idxs] * a[jdxs]
        li = int(np.argmax(contrib))
        gi = int(idxs[li])

        for f in factors:
            cand = best.copy()
            cand[gi] *= float(f)
            cand = normalize_sum(np.maximum(cand, 0.0))
            sc = internal_score_1(cand)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = sc
                best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def full_pipeline(a0, rng, deadline):
    """Run a full polish pipeline starting from a0."""
    best = normalize_sum(a0)
    best_sc = internal_score_1(best)

    def update(cand, sc):
        nonlocal best, best_sc
        cand = normalize_sum(cand)
        sc = internal_score_1(cand)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand
        return best, best_sc

    # Low-dimensional deterministic/random shape search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        c, sc = two_block_search(best, deadline)
        update(c, sc)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        tops = random_shape_search(best, rng, n_trials=120, keep=10)
        for sc, c in tops:
            update(c, sc)

    # Multi-scale block descent.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        c, sc = block_descent_multi(best, deadline)
        update(c, sc)

    # Flattening via Gauss-Newton CG.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 140.0:
        c, sc = cg_flatten_sequence(best, deadline)
        update(c, sc)

    # Log-softmax L-BFGS with a beta schedule.
    betas = (1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0)
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > max(0.0, deadline - 170.0):
            break
        c = run_log_lbfgs(best, beta, symmetric=False)
        update(c, internal_score_1(c))

    # A symmetric subproblem can help because the objective is invariant under reversal.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 130.0:
        c = run_log_lbfgs(best, 20000.0, symmetric=True)
        update(c, internal_score_1(c))

    # Final local cleanups.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        c, sc = micro_peak_sweep(best, top_lags=20)
        update(c, sc)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        c, sc = cg_flatten_sequence(best, deadline)
        update(c, sc)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        c, sc = micro_peak_sweep(best, top_lags=30)
        update(c, sc)

    return normalize_sum(best), internal_score_1(best)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing evaluation_sequence.
    Returns the best verified sequence within the 2400 second budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Load previous sequence if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals() and height_sequence_1 is not None:
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 256 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-9:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose working length.
    if parent is not None:
        n_parent = len(parent)
        if 1024 <= n_parent <= 12288:
            n_main = n_parent
        elif n_parent < 1024:
            n_main = 8192
        else:
            n_main = 12288
    else:
        n_main = 8192

    if parent is not None and len(parent) != n_main:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(parent))
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_main)
        parent = np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent).astype(np.float64)
        parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
        parent = normalize_sum(parent)

    print(f"Time budget: {budget:.0f}s, main length: {n_main}, parent available: {parent is not None}")

    # Initial seeds.
    prefix = build_flat_prefix(n_main)
    candidates = []
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(("parent", normalize_sum(parent)))
    candidates.append(("prefix", normalize_sum(prefix)))
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(("parent_sym", symmetrize(parent)))
    candidates.append(("prefix_sym", symmetrize(prefix)))

    # Verify a few seeds exactly.
    best_a = None
    best_exact = float("inf")
    for name, cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        val = safe_eval(cand)
        isc = internal_score_1(cand)
        print(f"seed {name}: internal={isc:.10f}, exact={val:.10f}, len={len(cand)}")
        if val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_a = cand.copy()

    # Fallback seed if direct evaluation failed.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_a = prefix.copy()
        best_exact = internal_score_1(best_a)

    # Build list of pipeline bases, preferring internal-best but keeping diversity.
    bases = []
    for name, cand in candidates:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        if all(len(cand) != len(b) + 0 or not np.allclose(cand, b, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10) for b in bases):
            bases.append(normalize_sum(cand))
        if len(bases) >= 4:
            break

    # Sort bases by internal score without using lambda: sort indices manually.
    base_scs = [internal_score_1(b) for b in bases]
    if len(bases) > 2:
        order = list(range(len(bases)))
        # simple selection sort by score
        for i in range(len(order)):
            mi = i
            for j in range(i + 1, len(order)):
                if base_scs[order[j]] < base_scs[order[mi]]:
                    mi = j
            order[i], order[mi] = order[mi], order[i]
        bases = [bases[i] for i in order[:2]]
        base_scs = [base_scs[i] for i in order[:2]]

    # Run pipelines on selected bases.
    for bi, b in enumerate(bases):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
            break
        print(f"Pipeline base {bi}: internal={internal_score_1(b):.10f}")
        out_a, out_sc = full_pipeline(b, rng, deadline)
        val = safe_eval(out_a)
        print(f"Pipeline base {bi} internal={out_sc:.10f}, exact={val:.10f}")
        if val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_a = out_a.copy()

    # If we still have time and have not beaten the target, polish the best exact sequence.
    target = 1.5027
    if (not np.isfinite(best_exact) or best_exact > target) and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        print("Extra polish on current best...")
        out_a, out_sc = full_pipeline(best_a, rng, deadline)
        val = safe_eval(out_a)
        print(f"Extra polish internal={out_sc:.10f}, exact={val:.10f}")
        if val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_a = out_a.copy()

    # Final micro-sweep before return.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        out_a, out_sc = micro_peak_sweep(best_a, top_lags=40)
        val = safe_eval(out_a)
        print(f"Final micro sweep internal={out_sc:.10f}, exact={val:.10f}")
        if val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_a = out_a.copy()

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if len(out) == 0 or (float(sum(out)) < 0.01):
        out = [float(x) for x in build_flat_prefix(64)]

    final_val = safe_eval(out)
    print("FINAL:", final_val, "length", len(out), "time", time.monotonic() - t0)
    return out
```
